# '>' a line said, '?' a question and the answer taken, '*' what the script did, '!' a problem, '+'/'-' a flag set or cleared, '=' a variable changed, '+ seen' a scene played to its end; a way after the first is told from the answer where it parts from those before it.

=== BattleFrontierGateToFightArea.General
from Attendant at 5,17 of BattleFrontierGateToFightArea
way 0
  Attendant> What would you like to hear about?
  Attendant> The Battle Frontier is five halls for Pokémon battles and nothing else: the Battle Tower, the Battle Factory, the Battle Hall, the Battle Castle and the Battle Arcade.
  Attendant> Each hall has rules of its own, and each is run by a Trainer of fearsome strength. We call them the Frontier Brains.
  Attendant> Please enjoy your visit to the Battle Frontier!
  ? What would you like to hear about? = THE FRONTIER
way 5, from answer 1
  Attendant> Please enjoy your visit to the Battle Frontier!
  ? What would you like to hear about? = NOTHING
way 4, from answer 1
  Attendant> Every hall keeps a board of your best streaks beside its counter, and stamps a print in your records when you beat its Frontier Brain.
  Attendant> Have a look at one whenever you'd like to see how far you've come.
  Attendant> Please enjoy your visit to the Battle Frontier!
  ? What would you like to hear about? = THE RECORDS
way 3, from answer 1
  Attendant> Battle Points are traded for prizes at the Exchange Service Corner, the booths in the middle of the grounds.
  Attendant> Items your Pokémon can hold, moves to teach them, and more besides.
  Attendant> Please enjoy your visit to the Battle Frontier!
  ? What would you like to hear about? = THE EXCHANGE
way 2, from answer 1
  Attendant> Win through a round and the hall awards you Battle Points, BP for short.
  Attendant> The longer your winning streak, the more each round is worth. Beat a Frontier Brain and there is a handsome bonus.
  Attendant> Please enjoy your visit to the Battle Frontier!
  ? What would you like to hear about? = BATTLE POINTS
way 1, from answer 1
  Attendant> In every hall you battle Trainer after Trainer without a rest at a Pokémon Center between them.
  Attendant> No items from your Bag, no Exp. Points and no prize money. Pokémon above level 50 fight as if they were level 50, and certain rare Pokémon may not enter at all.
  Attendant> No two of your Pokémon may be the same species or hold the same item.
  Attendant> Please enjoy your visit to the Battle Frontier!
  ? What would you like to hear about? = THE RULES

way 1.0.0, from answer 1
  Receptionist> What would you like to know?
  Receptionist> Which part shall I explain?
  Receptionist> Four Pokémon take the stage and are judged three times: on their looks, on their dancing and on their moves.
  Receptionist> The three count the same. Whoever does best over all three wins the contest's ribbon.
  Receptionist> Come and see me again whenever you'd like to hear more, or to enter!
  ? Would you like to enter a Pokémon in a contest? = INFO
  ? What would you like to know? = THE BASICS
  ? Which part shall I explain? = A CONTEST
way 1.0.4, from answer 3
  Receptionist> We look forward to seeing you and your Pokémon on our stage!
  ? Which part shall I explain? = CANCEL
way 1.0.3, from answer 3
  Receptionist> In the Acting Competition each Pokémon shows a move to one of three judges, four turns running. No move may be shown twice in a row.
  Receptionist> A judge gives more to a Pokémon the fewer others chose them. A move of the contest's own quality raises the judge's voltage, and filling it up earns a bonus.
  Receptionist> Come and see me again whenever you'd like to hear more, or to enter!
  ? Which part shall I explain? = ACTING
way 1.0.2, from answer 3
  Receptionist> In the Dance Competition each Pokémon leads in turn. The leader makes up steps on the beat, and the others repeat them half a bar later.
  Receptionist> A step right on the beat scores most. The wrong step scores nothing at all.
  Receptionist> Come and see me again whenever you'd like to hear more, or to enter!
  ? Which part shall I explain? = DANCE
way 1.0.1, from answer 3
  Receptionist> In the Visual Competition a Pokémon is judged on its condition and on how it is dressed up.
  Receptionist> You are given a theme and sixty seconds to put Accessories from your Fashion Case on your Pokémon. The better they fit the theme, the better the score.
  Receptionist> Come and see me again whenever you'd like to hear more, or to enter!
  ? Which part shall I explain? = VISUAL
way 1.3, from answer 2
  Receptionist> We look forward to seeing you and your Pokémon on our stage!
  ? What would you like to know? = CANCEL
way 1.2, from answer 2
  Receptionist> There are four ranks: Normal, Great, Ultra and Master. Any Pokémon may enter the Normal Rank.
  Receptionist> To go on to a higher rank, a Pokémon must have won the rank under it in the same contest.
  Receptionist> Come and see me again whenever you'd like to hear more, or to enter!
  ? What would you like to know? = THE RANKS
way 1.1, from answer 2
  Receptionist> There are five contests, one for each quality a Pokémon can show: Cool, Beauty, Cute, Smart and Tough.
  Receptionist> Enter the one your Pokémon's condition suits best. Poffins are what raise a Pokémon's condition.
  Receptionist> Come and see me again whenever you'd like to hear more, or to enter!
  ? What would you like to know? = THE CONTESTS

from the trigger at 8,11 of GalacticHQ4F
way 0
  Cyrus> ...So. You have come. You will have heard what I told them in the hall.
  Cyrus> Heh. A lie, naturally. Oh, I mean to make a new world, that much is true. But not for the likes of Team Galactic.
  Cyrus> A whole new world for myself alone: nothing less could ever be complete, or perfect.
  Cyrus> You have met my followers. Every one of them is as useless and as unfinished as the last. You know that as well as I do.
  Cyrus> I know what brings you here. It is written all over you. Uxie, Mesprit and Azelf. You want them saved.
  Cyrus> I have no further need of them. I am finished with them. If your heart aches so, take them. It spares me the trouble of disposing of them.
  Cyrus> And yet you are a remarkable specimen. Those Pokémon are nothing to you, and still you come all this way for them. Out of pity?
  Cyrus> Pity. Compassion. What pointless things to feel. They make no sense, and they come from the weakest part of a person: the heart.
  Cyrus> It was nothing but sentiment that brought you here. I will make you regret listening to it!
  Cyrus> I see. You are strong, and the root of that strength is the compassion you feel for Pokémon.
  Cyrus> What a waste. Feelings like that are illusions, and like every illusion they fade with the years, until death puts an end to them.
  Cyrus> That is why I threw all of mine away. ...But never mind. You will never understand the place I stand in.
  Cyrus> Still, you came here alone, and that takes courage as well as strength. This is your reward.
  > Lucas received the Master Ball!
  > Lucas put it away in the Poké Balls pocket.
  Cyrus> The Master Ball is the best Poké Ball there is. It catches any wild Pokémon, without fail.
  Cyrus> I have no use for it. Trainers like you make friends and partners of Pokémon. The others in Team Galactic use them as tools.
  Cyrus> I do neither. I make the power of Pokémon my own.
  Cyrus> If you want to save the Pokémon of the lakes, the warp panel ahead will take you to them.
  Cyrus> I am going to Mt. Coronet. At the summit of that mountain, what I am owed is waiting.
  Cyrus> That is where I will make my new world...

=== GalacticHQHall.Speech
from the trigger at 20,12 of GalacticHQHall
way -
  Looker> Psst! Here! Over here, my friend!
  Looker> All of Team Galactic, gathered in a single hall... What can it mean?
  Cyrus> Members of Team Galactic! Hear me! I stand before you today as Cyrus.
  Cyrus> This world of ours is a crude thing. In a word: unfinished. To live in it has always been a struggle, and it always will be.
  Cyrus> We humans are just as unfinished, and so are Pokémon. Because there is so much we lack, we fight, and we hurt one another... It is ugly.
  Cyrus> I hate it. That nothing is whole, that none of us is whole: I hate it with everything I am.
  Cyrus> The world ought to be whole. The world must change.
  Cyrus> Then who will change it? I will. I, Cyrus, and Team Galactic. Yes: every one of you.
  Cyrus> Together we dug through the myths and laid their secrets bare. Together we took the Pokémon of legend.
  Cyrus> And now Team Galactic holds the energy to change the world! The power of our dream is in our hands!
  Cyrus> Understand me, all of you! The world I have dreamed of for so long is about to become real.
  Cyrus> Those of you who go to Mt. Coronet, and those who stay behind: our tasks may differ, but our hearts beat as one.
  Cyrus> Glory to Team Galactic!
  Galactic Grunts> RAAAAAH!!
  Galactic Grunt> Master Cyrus! There's nobody like him!
  Looker> ...Phew. So that was Cyrus, the boss of Team Galactic. Quite a performance, no? And the man is only twenty-seven!
  Looker> But a new world, made for Team Galactic and for nobody else? It is absurd. My head, it spins.
  Looker> This "new world": what is it? This "unfinished" world: what is that?
  Looker> Very well. To look into such things is the work of the International Police. And I am of the International Police, in good standing. So the work is mine.
  Looker> Here we part, my friend. Take every care!
